Title: Origenis Opera Omnia, Tomus 2: in Evangelium Joannis Commentariorum, Pars II (Commentary on John, Greek)
Creator: Origen; edited by Carolus and Carol. Vincent. De la Rue (Maurists), newly revised by Carol. Henric. Eduard. Lommatzsch
Publisher: Berolini (Berlin): Sumtibus Haude et Spener, 1832
Publication Date: 1832
Rights: Public Domain
Language: grc (Origen's Greek text); lat (Latin translation and series where the Greek is lost, and Lommatzsch's notes)
Rights basis: The item's metadata carries Public Domain Mark 1.0 (licenseurl creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/), and the imprint year is 1832, so it is out of US copyright by date as well.
Source: Internet Archive item origenous-ta-heuriskomena-panta-t.-2-1832, fetched from the item's own OCR text file on 2026-10-26 by the Library thread.
Provenance note: Host OCR text, unchanged apart from this header. The title page was read in the OCR, and the text was read at three points.
Content note: in Evangelium Joannis Commentariorum, Pars II (Commentary on John, Greek). The OCR reads as Greek and Latin with occasional misreadings of accents and breathings and stray marks from the footnote apparatus. Check any quotation against the page image before it goes into a record. Volume 1 of this set (the first part of the Commentary on John) was not vendored: its host OCR is unusable noise.

7006 ἑαυτοῦ. Καὶ ὃ ἄνϑρωπος τῆς ἁμαρτίας, ὃ υἱὸς
